## 轉為整型 (影像分析工具) · 用法

**id**　5b764e948d5c7290b0-2

**english**　Int

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 數學分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/int.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/int.html

**text**　輸入值可為正數，也可為負數。
如果相比于截斷更希望采用舍入方式，則請在執行操作之前給輸入網格加上 0.5。
轉為整型工具與下舍入工具之間存在差別。 例如，對于以下兩個值，轉為整型工具總是截斷數字： 1.5 將變為 1 -1.5 將變為 -1 而對于相同的兩個值，下舍入工具將返回如下： 1.5 將變為 1.0 -1.5 將變為 -2.0 另一個差別在于下舍入工具輸出浮點值，而轉為整型工具僅輸出整數值。
整型網格值的最大支持范圍是從 -2,147,483,648（由 -231 確定的最小值）到 2,147,483,647（由 231 – 1 確定的最大值）。 如果將轉為整型用在浮點型網格上，且該浮點型網格具有所含值超出該范圍的像元，則這些像元在輸出網格中的值將為 NoData。
將分類（離散）資料作為整型網格存儲所占用的磁盤空間要明顯少于將同樣的信息作為浮點型網格存儲所占用的磁盤空間。 如有可能，建議使用該工具將浮點型網格轉換為整型。
如果輸入是多波段網格，則輸出也將為多波段網格。 該工具將在輸入中的每個波段上執行操作。
如果輸入是多維網格資料，則將處理所有變量的所有剖切。 輸出將是 CRF 格式的多維網格。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/int.html#usage

**text**　Int示例 1（Python 窗口）
該示例通過截斷方式將輸入值轉換為整型。

**text**　# Name: Int_Ex_02.py
# Description: Converts each pixel value of a raster to an integer by truncation
# Requirements: Image Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.ia import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/iapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inRaster = "gwhead"

# Run Int
outInt = Int(inRaster)

# Save the output
outInt.save("C:/iapyexamples/output/outint")

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/int.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Int，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入網格資料或常量值 [in_raster_or_constant]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

## 折疊水文面 (制圖工具) · 用法

**id**　5b79caedfc960e76d3-2

**english**　Collapse Hydro Polygon

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 概化工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html

**text**　該工具經過優化，可以折疊圖徵以使其適合在小比例下表達為線。 可以以不同的折疊寬度多次運行該工具，以創建適用于不同比例的輸出。 壓縮輸入面圖徵可能會產生非預期中心線，除非它們與輸入連接水文線圖徵相交。
工具性能取決于資料的清潔度。 在輸入線和面之間的水文網絡中重疊輸入面圖徵或斷開連接可能會導致意外結果。
輸入面中的孔洞或島將導致輸出中心線圍繞孔分支化。 為避免這種情況，請在運行此工具之前使用消除面部件 工具從輸入面中移除孔洞。
處理大型水文資料集可能會超出內存限制。 這種情況下，可以通過在制圖分區環境設置 中確定一個關聯的面圖徵類從而以分區方式處理輸入資料。 將按順序處理分區邊界定義的資料部分。 處理完所有分區后，將同時生成線和面圖徵類輸出。 根據系統的可用內存盡量減少分區數量，以降低分區對處理時間的影響。 有關詳細信息，請參閱使用分區概化大型資料集。
處理大型單個輸入面圖徵可能需要很長時間；在運行此工具之前應避免融合輸入面。 如果必須先融合面，請選中合并相鄰輸入面參數。 使用此參數，該工具將合并輸入面而不創建大型面，同時保持水文網絡中的正確連接。 輸出線將在原始輸入面邊界處分割。 為避免處理時間過長，如果輸入面非常大，則可能需要使用切分 工具將輸入面拆分為較小的圖徵。 使用簡化面 工具可減少折點數以提高性能。
當折疊寬度值設置為大于 0 的距離時，必須指定輸出面圖徵類。 輸出面將包含比折疊寬度寬的面。 如果只有部分面小于折疊寬度值，則面可能會部分折疊。 將為所有輸入面創建輸出線，包括那些大于折疊寬度值的面。 折疊寬度值為 0 將導致所有輸入面完全折疊，并且不會創建任何輸出面圖徵類。
輸出線圖徵類將包含 COLLAPSED 欄位。 此欄位將包含值 0，以對應于未折疊的面；以及值 1，以對應于已折疊的面。
當折疊寬度值設置為大于 0 的距離時，將使用折疊寬度容差 (%) 和最小長度參數來避免部分折疊面中的振蕩。 當面河流沿其長度方向間斷性折疊時，就會發生振蕩，并形成一串由單線河流連接的小型面湖泊。 將分析在折疊寬度容差百分比值內的部分折疊面的部分，并在確定是否折疊該部分面圖徵時考慮周圍環境。 如果正在分析的面部分短于最小長度值，則可以忽略折疊寬度以防止振蕩。 最小長度基于為面創建的中心線的長度。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#usage

## 折疊水文面 (制圖工具) · 用法

**id**　5b79caedfc960e76d3-3

**english**　Collapse Hydro Polygon

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 概化工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html

**text**　部分折疊的面將在與輸出線的連接點處錐化，以在折疊的面和未折疊的面之間創建平滑過渡。 每個錐形的長度由錐形長度百分比參數控制，該參數將錐形長度設置為錐形位置處部分折疊面寬度的百分比。 如錐形長度百分比值為 0，則可避免所有錐化。
輸出線圖徵類將使用用于創建它們的圖徵 ID 填充屬性欄位。 根據需要使用這些值將輸出圖徵連接到其相應的輸入。 InPoly_ID—一個數字，表示包含用于創建輸出線圖徵的面的面圖徵類的輸入順序。 名為 <out_line_feature_class>_InPoly_DecodeID 的表將保存在輸出位置，以將 InPoly_ID 值鏈接到輸入圖徵類的名稱。 如果在圖徵資料集中指定了輸出位置，則會在地理資料庫級別上創建更高級別的表。 InPoly_FID—用于創建輸出線的輸入面圖徵的 OBJECTID 值。 InLine_ID—一個數字，表示連接水文線圖徵類的輸入順序，該圖徵類包含連接到輸出線圖徵的圖徵。 名為 <out_line_feature_class>_InLine_DecodeID 的表將保存在輸出位置，以將 InLine_ID 值鏈接到輸入圖徵類的名稱。 如果在圖徵資料集中指定了輸出位置，則會在地理資料庫級別上創建更高級別的表。 InLine_FID—連接水文線圖徵的輸入的 OBJECTID 值，該圖徵連接到輸出線圖徵。 如果沒有連接水文線圖徵連接到輸出線圖徵，則該值將設置為 -1。 輸出面還將包括 InPoly_ID 和 InPolyFID 屬性，并使用用于創建它們的圖徵類和圖徵的 ID 對其進行填充。 如果使用多條輸入連接線創建單個輸出圖徵，則每個屬性中只會記錄一個 ID 編號。
線輸出圖徵包括以下欄位屬性，用于記錄有關相應輸入面圖徵的寬度信息。 寬度將以輸出空間參考的線性單位進行測量。 MIN_WIDTH—沿輸出線的最小面寬度 MAX_WIDTH—沿輸出線的最大面寬度 AVG_WIDTH—沿輸出線的平均面寬度
輸出線圖徵將連接到輸入連接線圖徵接觸輸入面圖徵的外部邊界的位置，除非在多個面圖徵之間共享面邊界。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#usage

- 工具參考

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明

- 折疊寬度 （可選）
- 要考慮折疊的輸入水文面的閾值寬度。 所有低于指定寬度的面都將被折疊。 默認值為 0，這將折疊所有圖徵。

## 折疊水文面 (制圖工具) · 參數

**id**　5b79caedfc960e76d3-10

**english**　Collapse Hydro Polygon

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 概化工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html

**parameter**　collapse_width_tolerance

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 折疊寬度容差 (%) （可選）
- 將分析其中圖徵的百分比容差，并且在確定是否折疊圖徵時將考慮周圍環境。 這是為了最大限度地減少折疊內的振蕩。 默認值為 20%。 僅當已指定折疊寬度參數值時才應用此參數。
- Double

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#parameters

## 折疊水文面 (制圖工具) · 參數

**id**　5b79caedfc960e76d3-11

**english**　Collapse Hydro Polygon

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 概化工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html

**parameter**　minimum_length

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 最小長度 （可選）
- 在輸出面圖徵類中保留面所需的最小長度。 最小長度基于為面創建的中心線的長度。 如果面的中心線長于折疊寬度但短于最小長度，則該面將不會包含在輸出面圖徵類中。 默認值為 0。 僅當已指定折疊寬度參數值時才應用此參數。
- Linear Unit

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#parameters

## 折疊水文面 (制圖工具) · 參數

**id**　5b79caedfc960e76d3-12

**english**　Collapse Hydro Polygon

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 概化工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html

**parameter**　taper_length_percentage

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 錐形長度百分比 （可選）
- 輸出面圖徵類和輸出線圖徵類中的面之間的連接長度將錐化。 此參數將錐化長度指定為連接位置處寬度的百分比。 如錐形長度百分比值為 0，則不會錐化。 默認值為 50。 僅當已指定折疊寬度參數值時才應用此參數。
- Double

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#parameters

- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

- out_line_feature_class

- 資料類型

**values**　
- merge_adjacent_input_polygons （可選）
- 指定在計算中心線之前是否合并相鄰輸入面。 MERGE_ADJACENT—在計算中心線之前將合并相鄰輸入面。 這是默認設置。 NO_MERGE—將根據未合并的輸入水文面計算中心線。
- Boolean

## 折疊水文面 (制圖工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　5b79caedfc960e76d3-31

**english**　Collapse Hydro Polygon

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 概化工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html

**text**　# Name: CollapseHydroPolygon_sample2.py
# Description: Select the rivers and collapse features that are
#               below 6 meters wide to produce centerlines.

# Import System Modules
import arcpy
arcpy.env.workspace = r"C:/data/Hydro.gdb"

# Setting Local Variables
in_features = 'WaterPolygons'
out_line_feature_class = 'HydroCenterlinesOut'
merge_adjacent_input_polygons = False # default is True or "MERGE_ADJACENT"
connection_features = 'WaterLines'
collapse_width = "6 Meters"           # default is '0 Meters'
collapse_width_tolerance = 20         # default is 20
minimum_length = "10 Meters"          # default is '0 Meters'
taper_length_percentage = 50          # default is 50
out_poly_feature_class =  'HydroPolyOut'

# Select only the Rivers
inputLyr = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(in_features,
                                             "inputLyr",
                                             "FTYPE = 'rivers'")

# Run Collapse Hydro Polygon
arcpy.cartography.CollapseHydroPolygon(inputLyr,
                                       out_line_feature_class,
                                       merge_adjacent_input_polygons,
                                       connection_features,
                                       collapse_width,
                                       collapse_width_tolerance,
                                       minimum_length,
                                       taper_length_percentage,
                                       out_poly_feature_class)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#derived-output

## 折疊水文面 (制圖工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 概化工具集

**english**　Collapse Hydro Polygon

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html

**id**　5b79caedfc960e76d3-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Collapse Hydro Polygon，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入水文面圖徵 [in_features]、輸出線圖徵類 [out_line_feature_class]、合并相鄰輸入面 （可選） [merge_adjacent_input_polygons]、連接水文線圖徵 （可選） [connecting_features]、折疊寬度 （可選） [collapse_width]、折疊寬度容差 (%) （可選） [collapse_width_tolerance]、最小長度 （可選） [minimum_length]、錐形長度百分比 （可選） [taper_length_percentage]、輸出面圖徵類 （可選） [out_poly_feature_class]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#parameters

**english**　Collapse Hydro Polygon

**chapter**

**id**　5b79caedfc960e76d3-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/cartography/collapse-hydro-polygon.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_features", "label_zh": "輸入水文面圖徵", "label_en": "Input Hydro Polygon Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "One or more feature layers containing hydro polygons."}, {"parameter": "out_line_feature_class", "label_zh": "輸出線圖徵類", "label_en": "Output Line Feature Class", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The line feature class containing the centerlines of the collapsed polygons. It contains centerlines of all input polygons including those that are not collapsed. This feature class has a COLLAPSED attribute that specifies whether the centerline feature represents a collapsed polygon."}, {"parameter": "merge_adjacent_input_polygons", "label_zh": "合并相鄰輸入面 （可選）", "label_en": "Merge Adjacent Input Polygons (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether adjacent input polygons will be merged before calculating the centerlines. Checked—Input hydro polygons will be merged before calculating the centerlines. This is the default. Unchecked—Centerlines will be calculated from input hydro polygons that are not merged."}, {"parameter": "connecting_features", "label_zh": "連接水文線圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Connecting Hydro Line Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "Input hydro line features that connect to the input hydro polygons to be collapsed. Line features will be created to maintain these connections."}, {"parameter": "collapse_width", "label_zh": "折疊寬度 （可選）", "label_en": "Collapse Width (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The threshold width of an input hydro polygon to be considered for collapse. All polygons below the specified width will be collapsed. The default value is 0, which will collapse all features."}, {"parameter": "collapse_width_tolerance", "label_zh": "折疊寬度容差 (%) （可選）", "label_en": "Collapse Width Tolerance (%) (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "A percentage tolerance within which features will be analyzed and the surrounding context will be considered when determining whether to collapse a feature. This is to minimize oscillations within the collapse. The default value is 20 percent. This parameter is applied only when the Collapse Width parameter value is specified."}, {"parameter": "minimum_length", "label_zh": "最小長度 （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Length (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The minimum length required for a polygon to be retained in the output polygon feature class. The minimum length is based on the length of the centerline created for the polygon. If the centerline of a polygon is longer than the collapse width but shorter than the minimum length, the polygon will not be included in the output polygon feature class. The default value is 0. This parameter is applied only when the Collapse Width parameter value is specified."}, {"parameter": "taper_length_percentage", "label_zh": "錐形長度百分比 （可選）", "label_en": "Taper Length Percentage (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The length that connections between polygons in the output polygon feature class and the output line feature class will be tapered. This parameter specifies the length of the tapering as a percentage of the width at the connection location. A taper length percentage value of 0 will result in no tapering. The default value is 50. This parameter is applied only when the Collapse Width parameter value is specified."}, {"parameter": "out_poly_feature_class", "label_zh": "輸出面圖徵類 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Polygon Feature Class (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The polygon feature class containing the portions of the input hydro polygons that are not collapsed. This parameter is applied only when the Collapse Width parameter value is specified."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.cartography.CollapseHydroPolygon(in_features, out_line_feature_class, {merge_adjacent_input_polygons}, {connecting_features}, {collapse_width}, {collapse_width_tolerance}, {minimum_length}, {taper_length_percentage}, {out_poly_feature_class})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_features、out_line_feature_class、merge_adjacent_input_polygons、connecting_features、collapse_width、collapse_width_tolerance、minimum_length、taper_length_percentage、out_poly_feature_class。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## Generate Aviation Chart Leads (Aviation Tools) · 匯總

**id**　5b7d61a68d1b6e68ee-1

**english**　Generate Aviation Chart Leads

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 布局工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/generate-aviation-chart-leads.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/generate-aviation-chart-leads.html

**text**　Creates text graphics at intersection points between a map frame boundary and line features.
Chart leads are text elements that are placed where a line feature intersects the edge of the map frame of an aeronautical chart (usually an Enroute chart). Chart leads indicate the next significant point for line features continuing past the map frame by displaying the name of the next designated point or Navigational aid (Navaid) along a particular route. The text to be displayed is based on the line features attributes. To symbolize and place the chart leads, the tool uses preferences. The preferences are configured using a specific interface and should be established before running the tool.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/generate-aviation-chart-leads.html#summary

**text**　This tool modifies the input data. See Tools that modify or update the input data for more information and strategies to avoid undesired data changes.
You can generate chart leads for all the line features in the map frame or selected features. The line features that need to be generated for chart leads are also configured in the preferences.
The preferences from the Preferences Table parameter value will define how the tool generates chart leads. Learn more about creating preferences
For the Preference parameter value, specify the chart lead preference.

## Generate Aviation Chart Leads (Aviation Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　5b7d61a68d1b6e68ee-16

**english**　Generate Aviation Chart Leads

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 布局工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/generate-aviation-chart-leads.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/generate-aviation-chart-leads.html

**text**　import arcpy

# Create a reference to the project file (.aprx) containing your layout
aprx = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject(r"c:\data\ICAO.aprx")
in_layout = aprx.listLayouts("ICAO_ENR")[0]

# parameters
in_mapframe = "Layers Map Frame"
in_preferences_table = r'c:\data\AIS.gdb\Preferences'
preference = 'Enroute_Low'

# Run GenerateChartLeads
arcpy.aviation.GenerateAviationChartLeads(in_layout, in_mapframe, in_preferences_table, preference)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/generate-aviation-chart-leads.html#derived-output

## Generate Aviation Chart Leads (Aviation Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 布局工具集

**english**　Generate Aviation Chart Leads

**id**　5b7d61a68d1b6e68ee-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/generate-aviation-chart-leads.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Generate Aviation Chart Leads，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Layout [in_layout]、Map Frame [in_mapframe]、Preferences Table [in_preferences_table]、Preference [preference]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/generate-aviation-chart-leads.html#parameters

## 110596：分析變量值的順序必須為 <value>。 · 解決方案

## 001607：距離值超過 30 度。 如果超過 30 度，弦距離將不精確。 · 描述

**id**　5b9185c567a156ce7d-1

**english**　001607: Distance values exceed 30 degrees. Chordal distances are not accurate beyond 30 degrees.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 1601-1700

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01601-01625-001607.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01601-01625-001607.html

**text**　如果輸入圖徵未投影（即，座標使用經緯度表示）或在輸出座標系設置為地理座標系時，或者在為一個具有地理座標系空間參考的圖徵資料集指定輸出圖徵類路徑時，距離將按照弦測量值計算。 但弦距離測量值僅可對小于 30 十進制度左右的測地線距離進行較好的估算。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01601-01625-001607.html#description

## 001607：距離值超過 30 度。 如果超過 30 度，弦距離將不精確。 · 解決方案

**id**　5b9185c567a156ce7d-2

**english**　001607: Distance values exceed 30 degrees. Chordal distances are not accurate beyond 30 degrees.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 1601-1700

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01601-01625-001607.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01601-01625-001607.html

**text**　請將資料投影到投影座標系統中，并輸入較小的距離參數值或者接受默認距離參數，然后再重新進行分析。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01601-01625-001607.html#solution

## 120299：由于多重共線性而無法對至少一個局部模型進行估計。 · 描述

**id**　5b9503d80a58016e48-1

**english**　120299: Unable to estimate at least one local model due to multicollinearity.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 120201-120300

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/120001-130000/tool-errors-and-warnings-120276-120300-120299.html#description

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/power.html#summary

## 冪 (影像分析工具) · 用法

**id**　5b9eaa8591e792234e-2

**english**　Power

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 數學分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/power.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/power.html

**text**　無論輸入值是何種類型，此工具的輸出網格資料始終為浮點型。
如果兩個輸入均為單波段網格，或者輸入之一是常量，則輸出將是單波段網格。
如果兩個輸入均為多波段網格，則該工具將針對一個輸入中的每個波段執行操作，并且輸出將為多波段網格。 每個多波段輸入中的波段數必須相同。
如果其中一個輸入為多波段網格，而另一個輸入為常量，則該工具將使用多波段輸入中每個波段的常量值執行運算，并且輸出將為多波段網格。
如果兩個輸入均為具有相同數量變量的多維網格資料，則該工具將對具有相同維度值的所有剖切片執行運算。 輸出將是 CRF 格式的多維網格。 輸入中的變量必須至少具有一個常見維度和一個常見維度值以供此工具進行處理，否則將發生錯誤。 如果兩個輸入均具有一個變量但名稱不同，請在運行工具之前取消選中匹配多維變量地理處理環境（在 Python 中設置為 arcpy.env.matchMultidimensionalVariable = False）。 如果其中一個輸入為多維網格，而其他輸入為常量，則該工具將使用常量值，對所有變量的所有剖切片執行操作，并且輸出將為多維網格。
在地圖代數中，此工具的等價運算符符號為“**”（鏈接）。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/power.html#usage

**id**　5b9eaa8591e792234e-14

**english**　Power

- 工具參考

**text**　# Name: Power_Ex_02.py
# Description: Raises the pixels in a raster to the power of the values
#              found in another raster
# Requirements: Image Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.ia import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/iapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inRaster1 = "degs"
inRaster2 = "cost"

# Run Power
outPower = Power(inRaster1, inRaster2)

# Save the output
outPower.save("C:/iapyexamples/output/outpower.img")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/power.html#return-value

## 冪 (影像分析工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 數學分析工具集

**english**　Power

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/power.html

**id**　5b9eaa8591e792234e-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/power.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Power，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入網格或常量值 1 [in_raster_or_constant1]、輸入網格或常量值 2 [in_raster_or_constant2]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/power.html#parameters

## Unified Facilities Criteria (Aviation Tools) · 匯總

**id**　5ba6fd0a29c5168e5c-1

**english**　Unified Facilities Criteria

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 機場工具集
- 障礙標識表面工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/ufc.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/ufc.html

**text**　Creates obstruction identification surfaces (OIS) based on the Unified Facilities Criteria (UFC) 3-260-01 that is prescribed by MIL-STD 3007. These surfaces provide planning, design, construction, sustainment, restoration, and modernization criteria for the United States Department of Defense. Surfaces are created as polygon or multipatch features.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/ufc.html#summary

## Unified Facilities Criteria (Aviation Tools) · 用法

**id**　5ba6fd0a29c5168e5c-2

**english**　Unified Facilities Criteria

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 機場工具集
- 障礙標識表面工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/ufc.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/ufc.html

**text**　The Input Runway Features parameter must be z-enabled.
The tool creates UFC surfaces in an existing polygon or a multipatch feature class. If a feature class is selected for the Target OIS Features parameter value, it must have a vertical spatial reference.
The size of the surfaces depends on the Wing Type, Service Type, Runway Class, and Flight Rule parameter values.
To create a .json file for the Custom JSON File parameter, use the CustomizeOIS.exe utility included in the Aviation Airports Product Data download available from My Esri.
The clear zone surface is attached to the primary surface for this tool. It is used to specify the areas on the ground, located at the ends of the runway. To change the Clear Zone Surface measurement value, use the CustomizeOIS.exe utility included in the Aviation Airports Product data files download available from My Esri.
The Airport Elevation parameter has a default value of 0. If you use an elevation value greater than 0, it takes precedence and overrides any existing airport elevation value read from the Input Airport Control Point Feature parameter.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/ufc.html#usage

## Unified Facilities Criteria (Aviation Tools) · 用法

**id**　5ba6fd0a29c5168e5c-3

**english**　Unified Facilities Criteria

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 機場工具集
- 障礙標識表面工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/ufc.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/ufc.html

**text**　The Input Airport Control Point Feature parameter can be used to supply x-, y-, and z-geometry for the Airport Elevation parameter, displaced threshold point features, or both. If displaced threshold points are included, surfaces will be constructed based on their x-, y-, and z-geometry instead of their corresponding Input Runway Feature parameter endpoint.
OIS tools are flexible and accept a variety of input and output feature class types. For more information, see the OIS Input and Output schemas.
The horizontal surface generated by this tool will be continuous across the multipatch feature. 舊版本： In ArcGIS Pro 2.6.x and earlier versions, the horizontal surface generated by this tool will be cut by the transitional or approach surfaces.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/ufc.html#usage

**text**　import arcpy

# Input Runway Feature Class
inFeatures = r"C:\data\ois.gdb\RunwayCenterline"

# Feature class that will contain the OIS surface
outFeatures = r"C:\data\ois.gdb\ObstructionIdSurface"

# Generate UFC OIS
arcpy.aviation.UnifiedFacilitiesCriteria(inFeatures, outFeatures, 'FIXED',
                                         'AIRFORCE', 'CLASS_A', 'INSTRUMENT')

## Unified Facilities Criteria (Aviation Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　5ba6fd0a29c5168e5c-33

**english**　Unified Facilities Criteria

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 機場工具集
- 障礙標識表面工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/ufc.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/ufc.html

**text**　import arcpy

# Input Runway Feature Class
inFeatures = r"C:\data\ois.gdb\RunwayCenterline"

# Feature class that will contain the OIS surface
outFeatures = r"C:\data\ois.gdb\ObstructionIdSurface"

customJSON = r"C:\data\customOIS.json"

# Feature class that will contain the Airport Elevation feature
inACPFeatures= r"C:\data\ois.gdb\AirportControlPoint"

# Generate UFC OIS
arcpy.aviation.UnifiedFacilitiesCriteria(inFeatures, outFeatures, 'FIXED',
                                         'AIRFORCE', 'CLASS_A', 'INSTRUMENT', 0, 0, 0,
                                         customJSON, inACPFeatures)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/ufc.html#derived-output

## Unified Facilities Criteria (Aviation Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 機場工具集
- 障礙標識表面工具集

**english**　Unified Facilities Criteria

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/ufc.html

**id**　5ba6fd0a29c5168e5c-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/ufc.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Unified Facilities Criteria，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Input Runway Features [in_runway_features]、Target OIS Features [target_ois_features]、Wing Type [in_wing_type]、Service Type [in_service_type]、Runway Class [in_runway_class]、Flight Rule [in_flight_rule]、Length of High Runway End Clearway （可選） [highend_clear_way_length]、Length of Low Runway End Clearway （可選） [lowend_clear_way_length]、Airport Elevation （可選） [airport_elevation]、Custom JSON File （可選） [custom_json_file]、Input Airport Control Point Feature （可選） [airport_control_point_feature_class]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/ufc.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_runway_features", "label_zh": "Input Runway Features", "label_en": "Input Runway Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input runway dataset. The feature class must be z-enabled and contain polylines."}, {"parameter": "target_ois_features", "label_zh": "Target OIS Features", "label_en": "Target OIS Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The existing output feature class that will contain the generated UFC surfaces."}, {"parameter": "in_wing_type", "label_zh": "Wing Type", "label_en": "Wing Type", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the wing type of the aircraft. If you specify Rotary, the Runway Class parameter will default to the Class A option without affecting the surface generation. Fixed—The wing type is fixed. Rotary—The wing type is rotary."}, {"parameter": "in_service_type", "label_zh": "Service Type", "label_en": "Service Type", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of military service. Air Force—The service type is Air Force. Army—The service type is Army. Navy—The service type is Navy. Marine Corps—The service type is Marine Corps."}, {"parameter": "in_runway_class", "label_zh": "Runway Class", "label_en": "Runway Class", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the runway class. Runways are classified as either Class A or Class B based on aircraft type. Class A—The runway classification is Class A. Class B—The runway classification is Class B."}, {"parameter": "in_flight_rule", "label_zh": "Flight Rule", "label_en": "Flight Rule", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the flight rule. These are the rules that govern the procedures for conducting flight, either instrument or under visual conditions. Instrument—The flight rule is instrument flight condition. Visual—The flight rule is visual flight condition."}, {"parameter": "highend_clear_way_length", "label_zh": "Length of High Runway End Clearway （可選）", "label_en": "Length of High Runway End Clearway (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The length of the area at the high end of the runway. The unit of measurement is based on the input runway features."}, {"parameter": "lowend_clear_way_length", "label_zh": "Length of Low Runway End Clearway （可選）", "label_en": "Length of Low Runway End Clearway (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The length of the area at the low end of the runway. The unit of measurement is based on the input runway features."}, {"parameter": "airport_elevation", "label_zh": "Airport Elevation （可選）", "label_en": "Airport Elevation (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The highest elevation on any of the runways of the airport. Provide the value in the vertical coordinate system linear units of the target feature class. If no value is provided, the highest point of the Input Runway Features parameter value will be used."}, {"parameter": "custom_json_file", "label_zh": "Custom JSON File （可選）", "label_en": "Custom JSON File (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "File", "description_en": "The import configuration, in JSON format, that will be used to create the custom OIS."}, {"parameter": "airport_control_point_feature_class", "label_zh": "Input Airport Control Point Feature （可選）", "label_en": "Input Airport Control Point Feature (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The point features containing an Airport Elevation feature, displaced threshold features, or both. Values provided for the Airport Elevation parameter will take precedence over these point features."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.aviation.UnifiedFacilitiesCriteria(in_runway_features, target_ois_features, in_wing_type, in_service_type, in_runway_class, in_flight_rule, {highend_clear_way_length}, {lowend_clear_way_length}, {airport_elevation}, {custom_json_file}, {airport_control_point_feature_class})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_runway_features、target_ois_features、in_wing_type、in_service_type、in_runway_class、in_flight_rule、highend_clear_way_length、lowend_clear_way_length、airport_elevation、custom_json_file、airport_control_point_feature_class。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

## 沿線生成矩形 (資料管理工具) · 用法

**id**　5bba1528bf8f4a6464-2

**english**　Generate Rectangles Along Lines

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 采樣工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/generate-rectangles-along-lines.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/generate-rectangles-along-lines.html

**text**　輸出具有以下欄位： ID—指定給各圖徵的增量編號。 GroupId—指定給相連線圖徵鏈或組中的所有圖徵的整數。 該工具結果可能包含多個組。 通常，單個組由一組相連的多個線圖徵組成或由帶有多個未相連部分的單條線組成。 基于未相連的獨立線圖徵創建的圖徵將分別指定給各個組。 GroupId 值唯一并且根據給定鏈中的第一個線圖徵的對象 ID 得來。 SeqId—根據各組中圖徵的創建順序指定給各圖徵的增量編號。 Previous—對各組中上一個 SeqId 值的引用，但各組中的第一條記錄除外。 第一條記錄可以參考組外的相鄰元素。 Next—對各組中下一個 SeqId 值的引用，但各組中的最后一條記錄除外。 最后一條記錄可以參考組外的相鄰元素。 Angle—值使用地圖的默認定向進行計算，其中 0 度表示正北方向，90 度表示正東方向，180 度表示正南方向，而 -90 度表示正西方向。
沿線長度和垂直于線的長度參數的默認單位將自動更改為輸入線圖徵的空間參考單位。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/generate-rectangles-along-lines.html#usage

**interface**　python

- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- spatial_sort_method （可選）
- 指定要用于創建輸出圖徵的順序起點。 將方向類型設置為右上方將啟動各輸入圖徵右上方的輸出圖徵。 UR—圖徵起點將為右上角。 這是默認設置。 UL—圖徵起點將為左上角。 LR—圖徵起點將為右下角。 LL—圖徵起點將為左下角。
- String

## 沿線生成矩形 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 采樣工具集

**english**　Generate Rectangles Along Lines

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/generate-rectangles-along-lines.html

**id**　5bba1528bf8f4a6464-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/generate-rectangles-along-lines.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Generate Rectangles Along Lines，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入線圖徵 [in_features]、輸出圖徵類 [out_feature_class]、沿線長度 （可選） [length_along_line]、垂直于線的長度 （可選） [length_perpendicular_to_line]、空間排序方法 （可選） [spatial_sort_method]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/generate-rectangles-along-lines.html#parameters

## 沿線生成矩形 (資料管理工具) · 英文版工具簽章與參數順序

**english**　Generate Rectangles Along Lines

- 工具參考

**id**　5bba1528bf8f4a6464-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/generate-rectangles-along-lines.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/generate-rectangles-along-lines.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_features", "label_zh": "輸入線圖徵", "label_en": "Input Line Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input polyline features defining the path of the features."}, {"parameter": "out_feature_class", "label_zh": "輸出圖徵類", "label_en": "Output Feature Class", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The output polygon feature class."}, {"parameter": "length_along_line", "label_zh": "沿線長度 （可選）", "label_en": "Length Along the Line (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The length of the output polygon features along the input line features. The default value is determined by the spatial reference of the input line features. This value will be 1/100 of the input feature class extent along the x-axis."}, {"parameter": "length_perpendicular_to_line", "label_zh": "垂直于線的長度 （可選）", "label_en": "Length Perpendicular to the Line (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The length of the output polygon features perpendicular to the input line features. The default value is determined by the spatial reference of the input line features. This value will be one-half the number used for the length along the line."}, {"parameter": "spatial_sort_method", "label_zh": "空間排序方法 （可選）", "label_en": "Spatial Sort Method (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the starting point of the sequential order that will be used in the creation of the output features. Setting the direction type to upper right will start the output features in the upper right of each input feature. Upper right—The starting point of the features will be the upper right corner. This is the default. Upper left—The starting point of the features will be the upper left corner. Lower right—The starting point of the features will be the lower right corner. Lower left—The starting point of the features will be the lower left corner."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.management.GenerateRectanglesAlongLines(in_features, out_feature_class, {length_along_line}, {length_perpendicular_to_line}, {spatial_sort_method})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_features、out_feature_class、length_along_line、length_perpendicular_to_line、spatial_sort_method。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 130178：<value>：不可為空欄位不能跟在可為空欄位后面。 · 解決方案

**id**　5bc34fb253c6aebbd7-2

**english**　130178: <value>: Non-nullable fields cannot follow the nullable ones.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 130101-130200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/130001-140000/tool-errors-and-warnings-130176-130200-130178.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/130001-140000/tool-errors-and-warnings-130176-130200-130178.html

**text**　如果允許某欄位使用空值，則也必須允許該欄位右側的所有欄位使用空值。 但是，如果選中了為空時填充復選框，則不必允許該欄位右側的欄位使用空值。

**content_version**　3.7

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/160001-170000/tool-errors-and-warnings-160526-160550-160534.html#solution

## 移除附件 (資料管理工具) · 匯總

**id**　5c010cb906b1db18c7-1

**english**　Remove Attachments

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 附件工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/remove-attachments.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/remove-attachments.html

**text**　從地理資料庫圖徵類或表記錄中移除附件。
由于附件實際并未存儲在輸入資料集中，因此不會對該圖徵類或表進行任何更改。 將對存儲附件和保持與輸入資料集的連接的關聯地理資料庫表進行更改。 匹配表用于識別哪些輸入記錄(或記錄的屬性組)將移除附件。
了解有關使用附件工具集中的工具的詳細信息

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/remove-attachments.html#summary

**id**　5c010cb906b1db18c7-2

如果不使用此工具，可選擇另一種方式，即從與輸入資料集值相同的地理資料庫的 InputDataset__ATTACH 表（用于存儲附件和保持與輸入資料集值的連接）中刪除所選記錄。
此工具支持將 ArcGIS Enterprise 托管圖徵圖層作為輸入。

- 匹配名稱欄位 （可選）
- 匹配表中的欄位，包含要從輸入資料集參數值記錄中移除的附件的名稱。 如果未指定名稱欄位，則會從匹配連接欄位參數值指定的每條記錄中移除所有附件。 如果指定了名稱欄位，但是記錄的名稱欄位值為 null 或為空，則將從該記錄中移除所有附件。 此欄位的值應為要移除的附件的簡稱，而不是用于制作原始附件的文件的完整路徑。

## 移除附件 (資料管理工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　5c010cb906b1db18c7-20

**english**　Remove Attachments

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 附件工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/remove-attachments.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/remove-attachments.html

**text**　# Delete unnecessary attachments from a feature class using a match table
import arcpy

input = r"C:\Data\City.gdb\Parcels"
input_field = "OBJECTID"
match_field = "MatchID"
name_field = "Filename"
workspace = arcpy.Describe(input).path

# Create a new geodatabase Match Table for the RemoveAttachments tool
# to delete the designated attachments for the associated ObjectID and file name.
match_table = arcpy.management.CreateTable(workspace, "remove_matchtable")
arcpy.management.AddFields(match_table, [['MatchID', 'LONG'], ['Filename', 'TEXT']])

# Create a list to remove attachments pic1a.jpg and pic1b.jpg from feature 1,
# pic3.jpg from feature 3, and pic4.jpg from feature 4.
delete_list = [[1, "pic1a.jpg"], [1, "pic1b.jpg"], [3, "pic3.jpg"], [4, "pic4.jpg"]]

# Iterate through the delete list and write it to the Match Table.
with arcpy.da.InsertCursor(match_table, ['MatchID', 'FileName']) as match_cursor:
    for row in delete_list:
        new_row = row[0], row[1]
        match_cursor.insertRow(new_row)

del match_cursor

# Use the match table with the Remove Attachments tool.
arcpy.management.RemoveAttachments(input, input_field, match_table, match_field, name_field)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/remove-attachments.html#derived-output

## 移除附件 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 附件工具集

**english**　Remove Attachments

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/remove-attachments.html

**id**　5c010cb906b1db18c7-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/remove-attachments.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Remove Attachments，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入資料集 [in_dataset]、輸入連接欄位 [in_join_field]、匹配表 [in_match_table]、匹配連接欄位 [in_match_join_field]、匹配名稱欄位 （可選） [in_match_name_field]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/remove-attachments.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_dataset", "label_zh": "輸入資料集", "label_en": "Input Dataset", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Table View", "description_en": "A geodatabase table or feature class from which attachments will be removed. Attachments are not removed directly from this table; they are removed from the related attachment table that stores the attachments. The dataset must have attachments enabled."}, {"parameter": "in_join_field", "label_zh": "輸入連接欄位", "label_en": "Input Join Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "A field from the Input Dataset parameter value that contains values that match the values in the Match Join Field parameter value. Records that have join field values that match the Input Dataset parameter value and the Match Table parameter value will have attachments removed. This field can be an Object ID field or any other identifying attribute."}, {"parameter": "in_match_table", "label_zh": "匹配表", "label_en": "Match Table", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Table View", "description_en": "A table that identifies which input records will have attachments removed."}, {"parameter": "in_match_join_field", "label_zh": "匹配連接欄位", "label_en": "Match Join Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "A field from the match table that indicates which records in the Input Dataset parameter value will have specified attachments removed. This field can have values that match the Input Dataset Object ID field or some other identifying attribute."}, {"parameter": "in_match_name_field", "label_zh": "匹配名稱欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Match Name Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "A field from the match table that contains the names of the attachments that will be removed from the Input Dataset parameter value's records. If no name field is specified, all attachments will be removed from each record specified in the Match Join Field parameter value. If a name field is specified but a record has a null or empty value in the name field, all attachments will be removed from that record. This field's values should be the short names of the attachments to remove, not the full paths to the files used to make the original attachments."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.management.RemoveAttachments(in_dataset, in_join_field, in_match_table, in_match_join_field, {in_match_name_field})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_dataset、in_join_field、in_match_table、in_match_join_field、in_match_name_field。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 001573: 邊界面區域中的點之間沒有足夠的差異。 可嘗試提供更大的邊界。 · 描述

**id**　5c05c9fe14b02bb64f-1

**english**　001573: There is not enough variation among the points within the bounding polygon areas. You can try providing larger boundaries.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 1501-1600

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01551-01575-001573.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01551-01575-001573.html

**text**　基于點位置和點數，工具將創建漁網網格以疊加點。 計算每個漁網方塊中的點數并移除邊界分析區域之外的方塊后，只剩下不到 30 個方塊。 該工具需要至少 30 個計數（30 個方塊），才能提供可靠結果。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01551-01575-001573.html#description

## 001573: 邊界面區域中的點之間沒有足夠的差異。 可嘗試提供更大的邊界。 · 解決方案

**id**　5c05c9fe14b02bb64f-2

**english**　001573: There is not enough variation among the points within the bounding polygon areas. You can try providing larger boundaries.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 1501-1600

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01551-01575-001573.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01551-01575-001573.html

**text**　如果點位于邊界分析區域中的多個位置，可能需要提供更大的面邊界。 如果點僅占據很少的唯一位置（如果有多個重合點），則比較好的解決方案為提供可疊加點的聚合區域。
另一種方案為指定分析欄位。 但是，問題將從哪些位置具有很多或很少的點變為高和低分析欄位值在哪些位置進行空間聚類。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01551-01575-001573.html#solution

**text**　指定的像元大小或從中獲取像元大小的資料集無效。 可以通過兩種方式設置像元大小。 要使用顯式值，它必須是正數。 如果要從現有網格獲取，則資料集必須存在于指定位置。

## 計算分割影像屬性 (影像分析工具) · 參數

**id**　5c1116d5b05afe434a-6

**english**　Compute Segment Attributes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 分類和模式識別工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/compute-segment-attributes.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/compute-segment-attributes.html

**parameter**　used_attributes

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 所用的分割影像屬性 （可選）
- 指定要包括在與輸出網格相關聯的屬性表中的屬性。 如果該工具中的唯一輸入是分割影像，則默認屬性為平均色度、像素計數、緊密度和矩形度。 如果還將附加輸入網格作為輸入與分割影像一起添加進來，則平均數字值和標準差選項也將可用。 聚合顏色—RGB 顏色值將基于每個分割從輸入網格獲取。 這也稱為平均色度。 平均數字值—基于每個分割，將從可選像素圖像中獲取的平均數字值 (DN)。 標準差—基于每個分割，將從可選像素影像中獲取的標準差。 像素計數—基于每個分割，構成分割的像素數。 緊密度—基于每個分割，決定分割為緊湊型還是圓形的度數。 值的范圍從 0 到 1，1 表示圓形。 垂直度—基于每個分割，決定分割為矩形的度數。 值的范圍從 0 到 1，1 表示矩形。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/compute-segment-attributes.html#parameters

**id**　5c1116d5b05afe434a-11

**english**　Compute Segment Attributes

- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- used_attributes [used_attributes,...] （可選）
- 指定要包括在與輸出網格相關聯的屬性表中的屬性。 如果僅對此工具輸入分割影像，則默認屬性為 COLOR、COUNT、COMPACTNESS 和 RECTANGULARITY。 如果還將 in_additional_raster 作為輸入與分割影像一起添加進來，則 MEAN 和 STD 選項也將可用。 COLOR—RGB 顏色值將基于每個分割從輸入網格獲取。 這也稱為平均色度。 MEAN—基于每個分割，將從可選像素圖像中獲取的平均數字值 (DN)。 STD—基于每個分割，將從可選像素影像中獲取的標準差。 COUNT—基于每個分割，構成分割的像素數。 COMPACTNESS—基于每個分割，決定分割為緊湊型還是圓形的度數。 值的范圍從 0 到 1，1 表示圓形。 RECTANGULARITY—基于每個分割，決定分割為矩形的度數。 值的范圍從 0 到 1，1 表示矩形。

## 計算分割影像屬性 (影像分析工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　5c1116d5b05afe434a-16

**english**　Compute Segment Attributes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/compute-segment-attributes.html#return-value

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/compute-segment-attributes.html

**text**　# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy.ia import *

"""
Usage: ComputeSegmentAttributes(in_segmented_raster, {in_additional_raster},
                               {used_attributes})
"""

# Set local variables
inSegRaster = "c:/test/moncton_seg.tif"
in_additional_raster = "c:/test/moncton.tif"
attributes = "COLOR;MEAN;STD;COUNT;COMPACTNESS;RECTANGULARITY"

# Run
compute_att = ComputeSegmentAttributes(inSegRaster, in_additional_raster,
                                       attributes)
#save output
compute_att.save("c:/test/moncton_computeseg.tif")

**chapter**

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/compute-segment-attributes.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Compute Segment Attributes，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入分割影像的 RGB 或灰階網格 [in_segmented_raster]、附加輸入網格 （可選） [in_additional_raster]、所用的分割影像屬性 （可選） [used_attributes]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**source_language**　en

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_segmented_raster", "label_zh": "輸入分割影像的 RGB 或灰階網格", "label_en": "Input Segmented RGB Or Gray Raster", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Raster Layer; Mosaic Layer", "description_en": "The input segmented raster dataset, where all the pixels belonging to a segment have the same converged RGB color. Usually, it is an 8-bit, 3-band RGB raster, but it can also be a 1-band grayscale raster."}, {"parameter": "in_additional_raster", "label_zh": "附加輸入網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Additional Input Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer; Mosaic Layer", "description_en": "Ancillary raster datasets, such as a multispectral image or a DEM, will be incorporated to generate attributes and other required information for the classifier. This raster is necessary when calculating attributes such as mean or standard deviation. This parameter is optional."}, {"parameter": "used_attributes", "label_zh": "所用的分割影像屬性 （可選）", "label_en": "Segment Attributes Used (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the attributes that will be included in the attribute table associated with the output raster. If the only input into the tool is a segmented image, the default attributes are Average chromaticity color, Count of pixels, Compactness, and Rectangularity. If an Additional Input Raster is also included as an input along with a segmented image, then Mean digital number and Standard deviation are available as options. Converged color—The RGB color values will be derived from the input raster on a per-segment basis. This is also known as average chromaticity color. Mean digital number—The average digital number (DN) will be derived from the optional pixel image on a per-segment basis. Standard deviation—The standard deviation will be derived from the optional pixel image on a per-segment basis. Count of pixels—The number of pixels composing the segment, on a per-segment basis. Compactness—The degree to which a segment is compact or circular, on a per-segment basis. The values range from 0 to 1, in which 1 is a circle. Rectangularity—The degree to which the segment is rectangular, on a per-segment basis. The values range from 0 to 1, in which 1 is a rectangle."}]

**signatures**　
- ComputeSegmentAttributes(in_segmented_raster, {in_additional_raster}, {used_attributes})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_segmented_raster、in_additional_raster、used_attributes。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## Generate Topographic Contours (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　5c121fa956cc34f626-2

**english**　Generate Topographic Contours

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html

**text**　This tool supports local raster files only.
The Area of Interest, Input Rasters, and Target Contour Features parameter values must have the same spatial reference.
If more than one input raster is used, all input rasters must have the same cell size, band count, and pixel type. All input rasters should also use the same elevation units (meters or feet).
The Area of Interest parameter value is used to limit the extent where the contours will be created. Typically, the Area of Interest parameter value will be a polygon feature that represents the extent of a map sheet at the specified scale. It will be buffered to ensure that contours extend to the full edge of the sheet and to reduce edge matching issues between sheets. Contours will only be created over the portions of the rasters that overlap with the extent of the buffered area of interest.
Use the Map Scale parameter to refine the appearance of the contours to be optimized for print and display at the specified scale. Specifying a scale sets the default contour values for several parameters based on the scale. The scale value also determines the minimum length of the contours and the smoothing tolerance that will be used to refine their appearance. When the scale and any of the parameters that calculate their default values from it are left blank, the tool will use the 1:50,000 scale to calculate the values. To clear contour values after they are automatically populated, choose the empty value at the top of the Map Scale drop-down list. Then specify the correct scale for the Map Scale parameter to repopulate the default contour values.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#usage

## Generate Topographic Contours (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　5c121fa956cc34f626-3

**english**　Generate Topographic Contours

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html

**text**　This tool also supports smoothing of the input rasters before creating the contour lines. When you set a Raster Smooth Tolerance parameter value, the tool uses the Adaptive Smoothing parameter from the Contour raster function to prepare the raster before generating contour lines. Depending on the tolerance used, the result can be contour lines that are smoother and more cartographically pleasing but less accurate.
This tool does not create a new output feature class; it adds the contours to an existing feature class. Most topographic agencies have defined the feature class, subtypes, and attributes appropriate for describing their elevation features in their existing data schema. Rather than creating a new output that must be mapped to that schema as a postprocess, this tool will add the results directly to the specified feature class.
A base contour is used, for example, to create contours every 15 meters, starting at 10 meters. In this case, 10 is used for the base contour, and 15 is used for the contour interval. The values to be contoured are 10, 25, 40, and 55.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#usage

## Generate Topographic Contours (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　5c121fa956cc34f626-4

**english**　Generate Topographic Contours

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html

**text**　Providing a base contour does not prevent contours from being created above or below that value.
If the raster contains negative elevation values, a zero contour will be included with the contours that are appended to the Target Contour Features parameter value. This occurs even if the optional Include Zero Contour parameter isn't checked.
If the Input Rasters parameter value contains an area with no data, such as a hole, the contours may intersect.
In addition to the contour elevation, you can populate the Contour Code Field parameter with information about the type of contour. When a Contour Code Field value is defined, use the following parameters to ensure that the field values are correctly populated: Contour Index Interval—Distinguish index contours from intermediate contours. Index Code—The value that will populate the Contour Code Field parameter value when the elevation of the contour is evenly divisible by the index interval and the contours are increasing in elevation. Intermediate Code—The value that will populate the Contour Code Field parameter value when the elevation of the contour is not evenly divisible by the index interval and the contours are increasing in elevation. Depression Code—The value that will populate the Contour Code Field parameter value when the elevation of the contour is evenly divisible by the index interval and the contours are decreasing in elevation. Depression Intermediate Code—The value that will populate the Contour Code Field parameter value when the elevation of the contour is not evenly divisible by the index interval and the contours are decreasing in elevation.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#usage

## Generate Topographic Contours (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　5c121fa956cc34f626-5

**english**　Generate Topographic Contours

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html

**text**　The tool supports various Topographic Mapping data model schemas. If the feature class provided as the Target Contour Features parameter value matches one of these schemas, the values for the Contour Height Field, Contour Subtype, Contour Code Field, Index Code, Intermediate Code, Depression Code, and Depression Intermediate Code parameters will automatically be set to the values appropriate for that schema.
The geometry of the contours can be further refined after they are created. Specifying a Contour Minimum Length parameter value will remove any isolated contours smaller than the length provided. For example, a contour line that encloses a peak but is only 10 meters long can be too short to be clearly visualized on a 1:100,000 scale map. Depending on the cell size of the input raster, the contour lines can have a coarse, blocky appearance. Use the Contour Smooth Tolerance parameter to apply a line smoothing algorithm to the contour lines to refine sharp angles and create smoother contours.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#usage

## Generate Topographic Contours (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　5c121fa956cc34f626-6

**english**　Generate Topographic Contours

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html

**text**　When using an input raster with elevation units in meters, map scales of 1:250,000 or smaller use a z-factor of 3.28084 to convert meters to feet. The resulting value is rounded up to the next highest 5-foot interval.
When using low-resolution input rasters, you can resample rasters to improve the contour output by checking the Resample Raster parameter. Generally, high-resolution input rasters do not need to be resampled.
Checking the Resample Raster parameter to process large Input Rasters values requires large amounts of disk space for processing.
Using a raster that only covers the target area helps lower processing times and mitigate memory issues. Smaller areas with a single raster take less computational effort to process than mosaics across a large area.
Do not resample a raster if its pixel depth is already high.
Use a midrange pixel depth if you are processing contours. Contour lines on a raster using a high pixel depth can cause issues. For example, if various loops or small tops with a 32-bit unsigned raster are visible, try using a 16-bit unsigned raster.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#usage

## Generate Topographic Contours (Topographic Production Tools) · 參數

**id**　5c121fa956cc34f626-13

**english**　Generate Topographic Contours

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html

**parameter**　scale

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Map Scale （可選）
- Specifies the scale that will be used to optimize contours. This is the scale of the cartographic product that will be printed. Specifying the scale will set the defaults of other parameters to values that are appropriate for the output scale. The default is 1:50,000. 1:5,000—The 1:5,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:10,000—The 1:10,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:12,500—The 1:12,500 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:25,000—The 1:25,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:50,000—The 1:50,000 cartographic product scale will be used. This is the default. 1:100,000—The 1:100,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:250,000—The 1:250,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:500,000—The 1:500,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:1,000,000—The 1:1,000,000 cartographic product scale will be used.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#parameters

## Generate Topographic Contours (Topographic Production Tools) · 參數

**id**　5c121fa956cc34f626-17

**english**　Generate Topographic Contours

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html

**parameter**　z_factor

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Z Factor （可選）
- The unit conversion factor that will be used when generating contours. The default is 1. The contour lines are generated based on the z-values in the input raster, which are often measured in units of meters or feet. With the default value of 1, the contours will be in the same units as the z-values of the input raster. To create contours in a unit other than that of the z-values, set an appropriate value for the z-factor. It is not necessary that the ground x,y and surface z-units be consistent for this tool. For example, if the elevation values in the input raster are in meters but you want the contours to be generated in feet, set the z-factor to 3.28084 (1 meter = 3.28084 feet).
- Double

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#parameters

- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Include Zero Contour （可選）
- Specifies whether a zero contour will be created. A zero contour represents sea level. Zero contours, when generated along a coastline, can be created inside a water body. Check the parameter if you want contours generated on land areas that are at or below sea level. 選中—A zero contour will be created. 未選中—A zero contour will not be created. This is the default.
- Boolean

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#parameters

- The amount of smoothing that will be applied to the contour lines. The larger the value, the more generalized the contours. The default is set by the scale value. If this parameter is set to 0 or left blank, no smoothing will be applied to the output contours.

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Supplemental Contours （可選）
- Specifies the interval at which a contour line will be placed between regularly spaced contours. This is used when the terrain change is not large enough to be depicted with consistent contour intervals. None—No supplemental contours will be created. This is the default. Half Auxiliary—Supplemental contours will be created at one-half the contour interval. Quarter Auxiliary—Supplemental contours will be created at one-quarter the contour interval.
- String

**parameter**　scale

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- scale （可選）
- Specifies the scale that will be used to optimize contours. This is the scale of the cartographic product that will be printed. Specifying the scale will set the defaults of other parameters to values that are appropriate for the output scale. The default is 1:50,000. 1:5,000—The 1:5,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:10,000—The 1:10,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:12,500—The 1:12,500 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:25,000—The 1:25,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:50,000—The 1:50,000 cartographic product scale will be used. This is the default. 1:100,000—The 1:100,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:250,000—The 1:250,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:500,000—The 1:500,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:1,000,000—The 1:1,000,000 cartographic product scale will be used.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#derived-output

**id**　5c121fa956cc34f626-43

**english**　Generate Topographic Contours

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html

**parameter**　z_factor

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- z_factor （可選）
- The unit conversion factor that will be used when generating contours. The default is 1. The contour lines are generated based on the z-values in the input raster, which are often measured in units of meters or feet. With the default value of 1, the contours will be in the same units as the z-values of the input raster. To create contours in a unit other than that of the z-values, set an appropriate value for the z-factor. It is not necessary that the ground x,y and surface z-units be consistent for this tool. For example, if the elevation values in the input raster are in meters but you want the contours to be generated in feet, set the z-factor to 3.28084 (1 meter = 3.28084 feet).
- Double

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#derived-output

This is the default.

**values**　
- supplemental_contours （可選）
- Specifies the interval at which a contour line will be placed between regularly spaced contours. This is used when the terrain change is not large enough to be depicted with consistent contour intervals. NONE—No supplemental contours will be created. This is the default. HALF_AUXILIARY—Supplemental contours will be created at one-half the contour interval. QUARTER_AUXILIARY—Supplemental contours will be created at one-quarter the contour interval.
- String

## Generate Topographic Contours (Topographic Production Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　5c121fa956cc34f626-60

**english**　Generate Topographic Contours

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html

**text**　# Name: GenerateTopographicContours_sample1.py
# Description: The Generate Topographic Contours tool creates contours that are
# appropriate for a cartographic product at a chosen scale. The following
# sample script demonstrates how to use Generate Topographic Contours to create
# contours appropriate for a 1:50,000 scale product using all the default values
# defined by the tool.

# Import System Modules
import arcpy

# Setting Local Variables
in_raster = r'C:\Data\Monterey.dt2'
aoi_fc = r'C:\Data\MapIndex.gdb\MapIndex\TLM50_Index'
contour_features = r'C:\Data\MGCP_TRD_4_4.gdb\MGCP_Delta\ContourL'
contour_subtype = 'CA010_Elevation_Contour_Line'
elevation_field = 'Highest Elevation'

# Create feature layer for area of interest and select a single feature based on the NRN field
aoi_layer = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(aoi_fc, 'aoi_layer')
arcpy.management.SelectLayerByAttribute(aoi_layer, 'NEW_SELECTION', "NRN = 'V795X16573'")

# Calling Generate Topographic Contours to create contours
arcpy.topographic.GenerateTopographicContours(in_raster, aoi_layer, contour_features, elevation_field, contour_subtype)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#derived-output

## Generate Topographic Contours (Topographic Production Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　5c121fa956cc34f626-62

**english**　Generate Topographic Contours

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html

**text**　# Name: GenerateTopographicContours_sample2.py
# Description: The Generate Topographic Contours tool creates contours that are
# appropriate for a cartographic product at a chosen scale. The following
# sample script demonstrates how to use Generate Topographic Contours to create
# contours appropriate for a 1:24,000 scale product using the 1:25,000 map
# scale value and overriding some of the default values.

# Import System Modules
import arcpy

# Set Local Variables
in_raster = r'C:\Data\Monterey.dt2'
aoi_fc = r'C:\Data\MapIndex.gdb\TOPO24K'
contour_features = r'C:\Data\Topo.gdb\Elev_Contour'
contour_subtype = '#'
elevation_field = 'ContourElevation'
scale = '1:25,000'
resample = 'RESAMPLE_RASTER'

# Default value for 1:25,000 scale is 10 m contours, change to 20 ft
contour_interval = 20
base_contour = 0

# Raster elevation is in meters, apply z_factor to get elevation in feet
z_factor = 3.2808
include_zero = 'ZERO_CONTOUR'

# Define the contour code field and values
code_field = 'FCODE'
index_interval = 100
index_code = '10102'
intermediate_code = '10101'
depression_code = '10105'
depression_intermediate_code = '10104'

# Adjust contour refinement values
raster_smooth = 0.2
minimum_length = '100 Feet'
contour_smooth = '25 Feet'

# Create feature layer for area of interest and select a single feature based on the Quad_Name field
aoi_layer = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(aoi_fc, 'aoi_layer')
arcpy.management.SelectLayerByAttribute(aoi_layer, 'NEW_SELECTION', "QUAD_NAME = 'Big Sur'")

# Call Generate Topographic Contours to create contours
arcpy.topographic.GenerateTopographicContours(in_raster, aoi_layer, contour_features, elevation_field, contour_subtype,
                                              scale, resample, contour_interval, base_contour, z_factor, include_zero,
                                              code_field, index_interval, index_code, intermediate_code, depression_code,
                                              depression_intermediate_code, raster_smooth, minimum_length, contour_smooth)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#derived-output
