## Generate Topographic Contours (Topographic Production Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**english**　Generate Topographic Contours

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html

**id**　5c121fa956cc34f626-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Generate Topographic Contours，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Input Rasters [in_rasters]、Area of Interest [area_of_interest]、Target Contour Features [contour_features]、Contour Elevation Field [elevation_field]、Contour Subtype （可選） [contour_subtype]、Map Scale （可選） [scale]、Resample Raster （可選） [resample_raster]、Contour Interval （可選） [contour_interval]、Base Contour （可選） [base_contour]、Z Factor （可選） [z_factor]、Include Zero Contour （可選） [zero_contour]、Contour Code Field （可選） [code_field]、Contour Index Interval （可選） [index_interval]、Index Code （可選） [index_code]、Intermediate Code （可選） [intermediate_code]、Depression Code （可選） [depression_code]、Depression Intermediate Code （可選） [depression_intermediate_code]、Raster Smooth Tolerance （可選） [raster_smooth_tolerance]、Contour Minimum Length （可選） [minimum_length]、Contour Smooth Tolerance （可選） [contour_smooth_tolerance]、Supplemental Contours （可選） [supplemental_contours]、Half Auxiliary Code （可選） [half_auxiliary_code]、Quarter Auxiliary Code （可選） [quarter_auxiliary_code]、Depression Auxiliary Code （可選） [depression_auxiliary_code]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-topographic-contours.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_rasters", "label_zh": "Input Rasters", "label_en": "Input Rasters", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Raster Layer; Mosaic Layer", "description_en": "The input raster layers that will be used to derive the contour lines."}, {"parameter": "area_of_interest", "label_zh": "Area of Interest", "label_en": "Area of Interest", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "A feature layer that will be used to clip the input raster before processing. A buffer is created before clipping the raster, which results in larger output contours that extend beyond the selected area of interest. The feature layer must have only one selected feature."}, {"parameter": "contour_features", "label_zh": "Target Contour Features", "label_en": "Target Contour Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "An existing line feature class or feature layer. Contours will be appended to this feature class."}, {"parameter": "elevation_field", "label_zh": "Contour Elevation Field", "label_en": "Contour Elevation Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field from the input contours that will store the contour elevation value. This field defaults to ZV2 or ZVH if a field with either of those names exists in the contour feature class."}, {"parameter": "contour_subtype", "label_zh": "Contour Subtype （可選）", "label_en": "Contour Subtype (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The subtype to which contours will be written if the input contours have subtypes."}, {"parameter": "scale", "label_zh": "Map Scale （可選）", "label_en": "Map Scale (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the scale that will be used to optimize contours. This is the scale of the cartographic product that will be printed. Specifying the scale will set the defaults of other parameters to values that are appropriate for the output scale. The default is 1:50,000. 1:5,000—The 1:5,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:10,000—The 1:10,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:12,500—The 1:12,500 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:25,000—The 1:25,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:50,000—The 1:50,000 cartographic product scale will be used. This is the default. 1:100,000—The 1:100,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:250,000—The 1:250,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:500,000—The 1:500,000 cartographic product scale will be used. 1:1,000,000—The 1:1,000,000 cartographic product scale will be used."}, {"parameter": "resample_raster", "label_zh": "Resample Raster （可選）", "label_en": "Resample Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the input raster will be resampled before creating contours. Checked—The input raster will be resampled before creating contours. Unchecked—The input raster will not be resampled when creating contours. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "contour_interval", "label_zh": "Contour Interval （可選）", "label_en": "Contour Interval (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The interval, or distance, between contour lines. This can be any positive number. The default is set by the scale value. If this parameter is left blank, the default scale value will be used."}, {"parameter": "base_contour", "label_zh": "Base Contour （可選）", "label_en": "Base Contour (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The value that contours will be generated above and below to cover the entire value range of the input raster. The default is 0."}, {"parameter": "z_factor", "label_zh": "Z Factor （可選）", "label_en": "Z Factor (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The unit conversion factor that will be used when generating contours. The default is 1. The contour lines are generated based on the z-values in the input raster, which are often measured in units of meters or feet. With the default value of 1, the contours will be in the same units as the z-values of the input raster. To create contours in a unit other than that of the z-values, set an appropriate value for the z-factor. It is not necessary that the ground x,y and surface z-units be consistent for this tool. For example, if the elevation values in the input raster are in meters but you want the contours to be generated in feet, set the z-factor to 3.28084 (1 meter = 3.28084 feet)."}, {"parameter": "zero_contour", "label_zh": "Include Zero Contour （可選）", "label_en": "Include Zero Contour (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether a zero contour will be created. A zero contour represents sea level. Zero contours, when generated along a coastline, can be created inside a water body. Check the parameter if you want contours generated on land areas that are at or below sea level. Checked—A zero contour will be created. Unchecked—A zero contour will not be created. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "code_field", "label_zh": "Contour Code Field （可選）", "label_en": "Contour Code Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field from the input contour feature class where the appropriate code will be stored. The default field is HQC if it exists in the input contour feature class."}, {"parameter": "index_interval", "label_zh": "Contour Index Interval （可選）", "label_en": "Contour Index Interval (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The interval, or distance, between index contour lines. For example, if the contour interval is 20 meters and you want index contours every 100 meters, specify 100. The default is set by the scale value."}, {"parameter": "index_code", "label_zh": "Index Code （可選）", "label_en": "Index Code (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The code value that will be stored in the Contour Code Field parameter value when an index contour is identified. The default code is 1 if the HQC field exists in the input contour feature class."}, {"parameter": "intermediate_code", "label_zh": "Intermediate Code （可選）", "label_en": "Intermediate Code (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The code value that will be stored in the Contour Code Field parameter value when an intermediate contour is identified. The default code is 2 if the HQC field exists in the input contour feature class."}, {"parameter": "depression_code", "label_zh": "Depression Code （可選）", "label_en": "Depression Code (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The code value that will be stored in the Contour Code Field parameter value when a depression contour is identified. The default code is 5 if the HQC field exists in the input contour feature class."}, {"parameter": "depression_intermediate_code", "label_zh": "Depression Intermediate Code （可選）", "label_en": "Depression Intermediate Code (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The code value that will be stored in the Contour Code Field parameter value when a depression intermediate contour is identified. The default code is 6 if the HQC field exists in the input contour feature class."}, {"parameter": "raster_smooth_tolerance", "label_zh": "Raster Smooth Tolerance （可選）", "label_en": "Raster Smooth Tolerance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The amount of smoothing that will be applied to the input raster before creating the contour lines."}, {"parameter": "minimum_length", "label_zh": "Contour Minimum Length （可選）", "label_en": "Contour Minimum Length (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The minimum length that will be used for an individual contour line. The default is set by the scale value. If the value is set to 0 or left blank, no contours will be deleted from the output contours based on their short length."}, {"parameter": "contour_smooth_tolerance", "label_zh": "Contour Smooth Tolerance （可選）", "label_en": "Contour Smooth Tolerance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The amount of smoothing that will be applied to the contour lines. The larger the value, the more generalized the contours. The default is set by the scale value. If this parameter is set to 0 or left blank, no smoothing will be applied to the output contours."}, {"parameter": "supplemental_contours", "label_zh": "Supplemental Contours （可選）", "label_en": "Supplemental Contours (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the interval at which a contour line will be placed between regularly spaced contours. This is used when the terrain change is not large enough to be depicted with consistent contour intervals. None—No supplemental contours will be created. This is the default. Half Auxiliary—Supplemental contours will be created at one-half the contour interval. Quarter Auxiliary—Supplemental contours will be created at one-quarter the contour interval."}, {"parameter": "half_auxiliary_code", "label_zh": "Half Auxiliary Code （可選）", "label_en": "Half Auxiliary Code (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The code value that will be stored in the Contour Code Field parameter value when a half auxiliary contour is identified. The default code is 3 if the HQC field exists in the input contour feature class."}, {"parameter": "quarter_auxiliary_code", "label_zh": "Quarter Auxiliary Code （可選）", "label_en": "Quarter Auxiliary Code (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The code value that will be stored in the Contour Code Field parameter value when a quarter auxiliary contour is identified. The default code is 14 if the HQC field exists in the input contour feature class"}, {"parameter": "depression_auxiliary_code", "label_zh": "Depression Auxiliary Code （可選）", "label_en": "Depression Auxiliary Code (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The code value that will be stored in the Contour Code Field parameter value when a depression auxiliary contour is identified. The default code is 22 if the HQC field exists in the input contour feature class."}, {"parameter": "river_lines", "label_zh": "", "label_en": "Input River Lines (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The river line features that will be used to identify points where rivers intersect the same contour line more than once or where a contour line intersects itself. Tip: You can also use this parameter to identify points where river or lake polygon features intersect the same contour line more than once by including Input River Polygons or Input Lake Polygons parameter values. Note: Use of this parameter creates two feature classes in the scratch geodatabase. The following hydrographic review feature classes are intended to identify contour anomalies for manual correction: directionOfFlowFC—Identifies points where river lines intersect the same contour more than once. selfIntersectFC—Identifies points where a contour intersects itself and points where the parameter values for Input River Polygons or Input Lake Polygons intersect the same contour more than once."}, {"parameter": "river_polygons", "label_zh": "", "label_en": "Input River Polygons (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The river features that will split contour lines where they intersect with the river polygon boundary."}, {"parameter": "lake_polygons", "label_zh": "", "label_en": "Input Lake Polygons (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The lake features that will prevent contour lines from being generated over them."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.topographic.GenerateTopographicContours(in_rasters, area_of_interest, contour_features, elevation_field, {contour_subtype}, {scale}, {resample_raster}, {contour_interval}, {base_contour}, {z_factor}, {zero_contour}, {code_field}, {index_interval}, {index_code}, {intermediate_code}, {depression_code}, {depression_intermediate_code}, {raster_smooth_tolerance}, {minimum_length}, {contour_smooth_tolerance}, {supplemental_contours}, {half_auxiliary_code}, {quarter_auxiliary_code}, {depression_auxiliary_code}, {river_lines}, {river_polygons}, {lake_polygons})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_rasters、area_of_interest、contour_features、elevation_field、contour_subtype、scale、resample_raster、contour_interval、base_contour、z_factor、zero_contour、code_field、index_interval、index_code、intermediate_code、depression_code、depression_intermediate_code、raster_smooth_tolerance、minimum_length、contour_smooth_tolerance、supplemental_contours、half_auxiliary_code、quarter_auxiliary_code、depression_auxiliary_code、river_lines、river_polygons、lake_polygons。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 應用錯誤解決方案 (公共設施網絡工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 遷移工具集

**english**　Apply Error Resolutions

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/apply-error-resolutions.html

**id**　5c20c392ac2f016a51-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/apply-error-resolutions.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Apply Error Resolutions，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入公共設施網絡 [in_utility_network]、解決方案表 [in_resolution_table]、輸出位置 （可選） [in_folder]、輸出名稱 （可選） [output_name]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/apply-error-resolutions.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_utility_network", "label_zh": "輸入公共設施網絡", "label_en": "Input Utility Network", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Utility Network Layer", "description_en": "The utility network that will be updated with the edit actions defined in the Error Resolutions table."}, {"parameter": "in_resolution_table", "label_zh": "解決方案表", "label_en": "Resolution Table", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Table View", "description_en": "The error resolutions table defining the edit actions that will be applied to the utility network classes to resolve errors."}, {"parameter": "in_folder", "label_zh": "輸出位置 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Location (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Folder", "description_en": "The output folder where the folder specified in the Output Name parameter will be created."}, {"parameter": "output_name", "label_zh": "輸出名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the output folder. This folder contains output .csv log files with information about features modified by the operation. When an edit fails, the logs provide information about any errors that could not be resolved."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.un.ApplyErrorResolutions(in_utility_network, in_resolution_table, {in_folder}, {output_name})

**text**　Registers point cloud or elevation point data to a Bathymetric Information System (BIS).

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/bathymetry/add-point-data-to-bis.html#summary

## Add Point Data To BIS (Bathymetry Tools) · 用法

**id**　5c3e544ff6730629ff-2

**english**　Add Point Data To BIS

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Bathymetry 工具箱
- BIS 管理

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/bathymetry/add-point-data-to-bis.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/bathymetry/add-point-data-to-bis.html

**text**　The Target Workspace parameter value must be a workspace that contains a BIS.
Inputs can be LAS, LASD, point feature class (z-enabled and not z-enabled), shapefile, or multipoint features.
Multiple inputs can be added to the BIS at the same time; however, these inputs should share the same vertical units, directionality, and cell size properties. The tool applies the input values for these properties to all data added in a single tool run.
The Cell Size parameter is used to generate proxy rasters from the point data.
Do not combine data types in a single tool run; doing so can cause unexpected results or failure.
Proxy rasters are stored in the proxy raster location specified in the BisDetails table.
Refresh the layers on the List By Data Source tab of the Contents pane to see datasets added by other users when adding data to an enterprise geodatabase BIS.
The output footprints will use the coordinate system of the BIS. If the input data has a different coordinate system than the BIS, use the Geographic Transformations environment with this tool. Learn more about geographic datum transformations

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/bathymetry/add-point-data-to-bis.html#usage

**id**　5c3e544ff6730629ff-9

**english**　Add Point Data To BIS

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Bathymetry 工具箱
- BIS 管理

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/bathymetry/add-point-data-to-bis.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/bathymetry/add-point-data-to-bis.html

**parameter**　footprint_type

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Footprint Type （可選）
- Specifies whether the footprint will be the full extent of the dataset, a convex hull representing the minimum bounding box for all features, or a precise domain representing the approximate boundary of the input dataset. Envelope—The footprint will be the full extent of the dataset. This is the default. Convex Hull—The footprint will be a convex polygon representing the minimum bounding box for all features. Precise Domain—The footprint will be a polygon that is the approximate boundary of the input dataset.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/bathymetry/add-point-data-to-bis.html#parameters

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- footprint_type （可選）
- Specifies whether the footprint will be the full extent of the dataset, a convex hull representing the minimum bounding box for all features, or a precise domain representing the approximate boundary of the input dataset. ENVELOPE—The footprint will be the full extent of the dataset. This is the default. CONVEX_HULL—The footprint will be a convex polygon representing the minimum bounding box for all features. PRECISE_DOMAIN—The footprint will be a polygon that is the approximate boundary of the input dataset.
- String

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/bathymetry/add-point-data-to-bis.html#derived-output

## Add Point Data To BIS (Bathymetry Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　5c3e544ff6730629ff-24

**english**　Add Point Data To BIS

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Bathymetry 工具箱
- BIS 管理

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/bathymetry/add-point-data-to-bis.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/bathymetry/add-point-data-to-bis.html

**text**　# Import arcpy module
import arcpy

# Identify tool parameters
target_workspace = r"C:\BIS.gdb"
in_data = r"C:\Data\surveys.gdb\survey01; C:\Data\surveys.gdb\survey02; C:\Data\surveys.gdb\survey03"
vertical_units = "FEET"
directionality = "POSITIVE_DOWN"
depth_field = "Z_depth"
footprint_type = "ENVELOPE"
cell_size = ""
update_overviews = "UPDATE_OVERVIEWS"

# Run the tool
updated_bis = arcpy.bathymetry.AddPointDataToBIS(target_workspace, in_data, vertical_units, directionality, depth_field, footprint_type, cell_size, update_overviews)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/bathymetry/add-point-data-to-bis.html#derived-output

## Add Point Data To BIS (Bathymetry Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Bathymetry 工具箱
- BIS 管理

**english**　Add Point Data To BIS

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/bathymetry/add-point-data-to-bis.html

**id**　5c3e544ff6730629ff-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/bathymetry/add-point-data-to-bis.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Add Point Data To BIS，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Target Workspace [target_workspace]、Input Data [in_data]、Vertical Units [vertical_units]、Directionality [directionality]、Depth Field （可選） [depth_field]、Footprint Type （可選） [footprint_type]、Cell Size （可選） [cell_size]、Update Overviews （可選） [update_overviews]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/bathymetry/add-point-data-to-bis.html#parameters

Fathoms—The vertical units will be fathoms."}, {"parameter": "directionality", "label_zh": "Directionality", "label_en": "Directionality", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the directionality of the elevation data. Positive Up—The directionality of the elevation data will be positive up. Positive Down—The directionality of the elevation data will be positive down."}, {"parameter": "depth_field", "label_zh": "Depth Field （可選）", "label_en": "Depth Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The field where the depth is stored. It is either a numeric field or the shape field specified in the Input Data parameter value."}, {"parameter": "footprint_type", "label_zh": "Footprint Type （可選）", "label_en": "Footprint Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether the footprint will be the full extent of the dataset, a convex hull representing the minimum bounding box for all features, or a precise domain representing the approximate boundary of the input dataset. Envelope—The footprint will be the full extent of the dataset. This is the default. Convex Hull—The footprint will be a convex polygon representing the minimum bounding box for all features. Precise Domain—The footprint will be a polygon that is the approximate boundary of the input dataset."}, {"parameter": "cell_size", "label_zh": "Cell Size （可選）", "label_en": "Cell Size (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The cell size of the proxy raster. The horizontal unit is the same as that of the input datasets. If no cell size is specified, additional rules will be used to calculate it from the other inputs."}, {"parameter": "update_overviews", "label_zh": "Update Overviews （可選）", "label_en": "Update Overviews (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether overviews and statistics for a mosaic dataset will be calculated and updated. Checked—Overviews and statistics will be calculated and updated. This is the default. Unchecked—Overviews and statistics will not be calculated and updated."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.bathymetry.AddPointDataToBIS(target_workspace, in_data, vertical_units, directionality, {depth_field}, {footprint_type}, {cell_size}, {update_overviews})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：target_workspace、in_data、vertical_units、directionality、depth_field、footprint_type、cell_size、update_overviews。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 創建 OD 成本矩陣分析圖層 (Network Analyst 工具) · 匯總

**id**　5c40e3e310f9c6065f-1

**english**　Make OD Cost Matrix Analysis Layer

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html

**text**　創建 起點-目的地 (OD) 成本矩陣網絡分析圖層 并設置其分析屬性。 OD 成本矩陣分析圖層對于描述從一組起始位置到一組目的地位置的成本矩陣十分有用。 該圖層可通過本地網絡資料集進行創建，也可通過在線托管服務或門戶托管服務進行創建。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html#summary

## 創建 OD 成本矩陣分析圖層 (Network Analyst 工具) · 用法

**id**　5c40e3e310f9c6065f-2

**english**　Make OD Cost Matrix Analysis Layer

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html

**text**　該工具通常用作網絡分析工作流的第一步。 圖層創建后，您可以使用添加位置工具將輸入添加到圖層，并使用求解工具求解分析。 了解有關網絡分析圖層工作流的詳細信息
當使用 ArcGIS Online 或使用 ArcGIS Online 配置路徑服務的 ArcGIS Enterprise 門戶作為輸入網絡資料源時，解決分析將消耗配額并受到某些限制，例如允許的輸入數量。 了解關于網絡分析中配額消耗和分析限制的詳細信息
默認情況下，將在當前工作空間地理處理環境（通常為工程地理資料庫）中新建的、自動命名的圖徵資料集中創建新分析圖層的資料。 將在相同地理資料庫的根級別創建基于表的資料，因為無法將表存儲在圖徵資料集中。 可以使用分析地理資料庫和分析圖徵資料集名稱參數來覆蓋此行為。
雖然該工具可以在 Python 腳本中使用，但在 Python 中執行網絡分析的最佳實踐是使用 arcpy.nax 模塊中的求解程序類。 了解有關在 Python 中執行網絡分析的詳細信息。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html#usage

## 創建 OD 成本矩陣分析圖層 (Network Analyst 工具) · 參數

**id**　5c40e3e310f9c6065f-11

**english**　Make OD Cost Matrix Analysis Layer

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html

**parameter**　line_shape

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 線狀 （可選）
- 指定輸出線形狀。 無論指定的輸出形狀類型為何，最佳路徑始終由網絡阻抗（而非歐氏距離）決定。 這表示只是路徑形狀不同，而對網絡進行的基礎遍歷則相同。 無線—將不會為輸出起始點-目的地路徑對生成任何形狀。 適用于存在大量起始點和目的地，但僅對 OD 成本矩陣表中的阻抗成本（而不是查看地圖中的 OD 成本矩陣）感興趣的情況。 直線—輸出路徑形狀是介于各個起始點-目的地對之間的直線（單線）。 這是默認設置。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html#parameters

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 累積屬性 （可選）
- 分析過程中要累積的成本屬性的列表。 這些累積屬性僅供參考；求解程序僅使用求解分析時指定的出行模式所使用的成本屬性。 對于每個累積的成本屬性，會在網絡分析輸出圖徵中填充 Total_ [阻抗] 屬性。 如果網絡資料源為 ArcGIS Online 服務，或如果網絡資料源是不支持累積的 Portal for ArcGIS 版本上的服務，則此參數不可用。
- String

- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 在求解時忽略無效的位置 （可選）
- 指定是否忽略無效的輸入位置。 通常，如果無法 在網絡上定位，則位置無效。 當無效位置被忽略時，求解程序將跳過它們并嘗試使用剩余位置執行分析。 選中—將忽略無效輸入位置并僅使用有效位置。 這是默認設置。 未選中—將使用所有輸入位置。 無效位置將導致分析失敗。
- Boolean

**english**　Make OD Cost Matrix Analysis Layer

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**parameter**　line_shape

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- line_shape （可選）
- 指定輸出線形狀。 無論指定的輸出形狀類型為何，最佳路徑始終由網絡阻抗（而非歐氏距離）決定。 這表示只是路徑形狀不同，而對網絡進行的基礎遍歷則相同。 NO_LINES—將不會為輸出起始點-目的地路徑對生成任何形狀。 適用于存在大量起始點和目的地，但僅對 OD 成本矩陣表中的阻抗成本（而不是查看地圖中的 OD 成本矩陣）感興趣的情況。 STRAIGHT_LINES—輸出路徑形狀是介于各個起始點-目的地對之間的直線（單線）。 這是默認設置。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html#derived-output

- 資料類型

**values**　
- accumulate_attributes [accumulate_attributes,...] （可選）
- 分析過程中要累積的成本屬性的列表。 這些累積屬性僅供參考；求解程序僅使用求解分析時指定的出行模式所使用的成本屬性。 對于每個累積的成本屬性，會在網絡分析輸出圖徵中填充 Total_ [阻抗] 屬性。 如果網絡資料源為 ArcGIS Online 服務，或如果網絡資料源是不支持累積的 Portal for ArcGIS 版本上的服務，則此參數不可用。
- String

- 說明

- ignore_invalid_locations （可選）
- 指定是否忽略無效的輸入位置。 通常，如果無法 在網絡上定位，則位置無效。 當無效位置被忽略時，求解程序將跳過它們并嘗試使用剩余位置執行分析。 SKIP—將忽略無效輸入位置并僅使用有效位置。 這是默認設置。 HALT—將使用所有輸入位置。 無效位置將導致分析失敗。
- Boolean

## 創建 OD 成本矩陣分析圖層 (Network Analyst 工具) · 提示：

**id**　5c40e3e310f9c6065f-36

**english**　Make OD Cost Matrix Analysis Layer

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html

**text**　# Name: MakeODCostMatrixAnalysisLayer_Workflow.py
# Description: Create an origin-destination cost matrix for delivery of goods
#              from the warehouses to all stores within a 10-minute drive time
#              and save the results to a layer file on disk. Such a matrix can
#              be used as an input for logistics, delivery and routing analyses.
# Requirements: Network Analyst extension

#Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
import os

try:
    #Set environment settings
    output_dir = "C:/Data"
    #The NA layer's data will be saved to the workspace specified here
    env.workspace = os.path.join(output_dir, "Output.gdb")
    env.overwriteOutput = True

    #Set local variables
    input_gdb = "C:/Data/Paris.gdb"
    network = os.path.join(input_gdb, "Transportation", "ParisMultimodal_ND")
    layer_name = "WarehouseToStoreDrivetimeMatrix"
    travel_mode = "Driving Time"
    search_tolerance = "1000 Meters"
    origins = os.path.join(input_gdb, "Analysis", "Warehouses")
    destinations = os.path.join(input_gdb, "Analysis", "Stores")
    output_layer_file = os.path.join(output_dir, layer_name + ".lyrx")

    #Create a new OD Cost matrix layer. We wish to find all stores within a 10
    #minute cutoff.
    result_object = arcpy.na.MakeODCostMatrixAnalysisLayer(network, layer_name,
                                                travel_mode, 10)

    #Get the layer object from the result object. The OD cost matrix layer can
    #now be referenced using the layer object.
    layer_object = result_object.getOutput(0)

    #Get the names of all the sublayers within the OD cost matrix layer.
    sublayer_names = arcpy.na.GetNAClassNames(layer_object)
    #Stores the layer names that we will use later
    origins_layer_name = sublayer_names["Origins"]
    destinations_layer_name = sublayer_names["Destinations"]

    #Load the warehouse locations as origins using a default field mappings and
    #a search tolerance of 1000 Meters.
    arcpy.na.AddLocations(layer_object, origins_layer_name, origins, "",
                          search_tolerance)

    #Load the store locations as destinations and map the NOM field from stores
    #features as Name property using field mappings
    field_mappings = arcpy.na.NAClassFieldMappings(layer_object,
                                                        destinations_layer_name)
    field_mappings["Name"].mappedFieldName = "NOM"
    arcpy.na.AddLocations(layer_object, destinations_layer_name, destinations,
                          field_mappings, search_tolerance)

    #Solve the OD cost matrix layer
    arcpy.na.Solve(layer_object)

    #Save the solved OD cost matrix layer as a layer file on disk
    layer_object.saveACopy(output_layer_file)

    print("Script completed successfully")

except Exception as e:
    # If an error occurred, print line number and error message
    import traceback, sys
    tb = sys.exc_info()[2]
    print("An error occurred on line %i" % tb.tb_lineno)
    print(str(e))

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html#derived-output

## 創建 OD 成本矩陣分析圖層 (Network Analyst 工具) · 舊版本：

**id**　5c40e3e310f9c6065f-39

**english**　Make OD Cost Matrix Analysis Layer

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html

**text**　# Name: MakeODCostMatrixAnalysisLayer_Workflow2.py
# Description: Find the travel time to the closest hospital from each census
#               tract and join the travel time and hospital name to the input
#               tracts.
# Requirements: Network Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import datetime
import os
import arcpy

try:
    # Set environment settings
    output_dir = "C:/Data"
    #The NA layer's data will be saved to the workspace specified here
    arcpy.env.workspace = os.path.join(output_dir, "Output.gdb")
    arcpy.env.overwriteOutput = True

    # Set inputs and outputs
    input_gdb = "C:/Data/SanFrancisco.gdb"
    network = os.path.join(input_gdb, "Transportation", "Streets_ND")
    origins = os.path.join(input_gdb, "Analysis", "TractCentroids")
    destinations = os.path.join(input_gdb, "Analysis", "Hospitals")
    output_features = "TractCentroids_withOD"

    # Define some OD cost matrix analysis settings
    layer_name = "HospitalsOD"
    # User settings for driving
    travel_mode = "Driving Time"
    # Calculate the total distance, even though the analysis is optimizing time
    accumulate_attributes = ["Meters"]
    # Find only the closest hospital
    num_hospitals_to_find = 1
    # Set the time of day for the analysis to 6PM on a generic Monday.
    start_time = datetime.datetime(1900, 1, 1, 18, 0, 0)
    # Don't output line shapes (output Lines will still list travel times)
    out_lines = "NO_LINES"

    # Create a new OD cost matrix layer.
    result_object = arcpy.na.MakeODCostMatrixAnalysisLayer(network, layer_name,
                    travel_mode,
                    number_of_destinations_to_find=num_hospitals_to_find,
                    time_of_day=start_time, line_shape=out_lines,
                    accumulate_attributes=accumulate_attributes)

    # Get the layer object from the result object. The OD layer can
    # now be referenced using the layer object.
    layer_object = result_object.getOutput(0)

    # Get the names of all the sublayers within the OD layer.
    sublayer_names = arcpy.na.GetNAClassNames(layer_object)
    # Store the layer names for later use
    origins_layer_name = sublayer_names["Origins"]
    destinations_layer_name = sublayer_names["Destinations"]

    # The input census tract data has a unique ID field that can be transferred
    # to the analysis layer. Add the field, and then use field mapping to
    # transfer the values.
    arcpy.na.AddFieldToAnalysisLayer(layer_object, origins_layer_name,
                                                        "Tract_ID", "TEXT")
    field_mappings = arcpy.na.NAClassFieldMappings(layer_object,
                                                            origins_layer_name)
    field_mappings["Tract_ID"].mappedFieldName = "ID"

    # Load the census tracts as origins.
    arcpy.na.AddLocations(layer_object, origins_layer_name, origins,
                            field_mappings, "")

    # Map the input hospital NAME field to a new Hospital_Name field in
    # Destinations
    arcpy.na.AddFieldToAnalysisLayer(layer_object, destinations_layer_name,
                                                        "Hospital_Name", "TEXT")
    field_mappings = arcpy.na.NAClassFieldMappings(layer_object,
                                                        destinations_layer_name)
    field_mappings["Hospital_Name"].mappedFieldName = "NAME"

    # Load the hospitals as desinations.
    arcpy.na.AddLocations(layer_object, destinations_layer_name, destinations,
                            field_mappings, "")

    # Solve the OD layer
    arcpy.na.Solve(layer_object)

    # Get sublayers
    origins_sublayer = arcpy.na.GetNASublayer(layer_object, "Origins")
    destinations_sublayer = arcpy.na.GetNASublayer(layer_object, "Destinations")
    lines_sublayer = arcpy.na.GetNASublayer(layer_object, "ODLines")

    # Use the JoinField tool to transfer OD Cost Matrix information to the
    # output feature class
    # Transfer the tract ID from the input Origins to the output Lines
    arcpy.management.JoinField(lines_sublayer, "OriginID",
                                    origins_sublayer, "ObjectID", "Tract_ID")
    # Transfer the hospital name from the input Destinations to the output Lines
    arcpy.management.JoinField(lines_sublayer, "DestinationID",
                            destinations_sublayer, "ObjectID", "Hospital_Name")
    # Transfer fields of interest (hospital name, impedance attribute, and other
    # accumulated costs) from the output Lines to a copy of the input census
    # tracts feature class using the Tract_ID field
    # Determine the impedance attribute
    solver_props = arcpy.na.GetSolverProperties(layer_object)
    impedance = solver_props.impedance
    output_impedance_fieldname = "Total_" + impedance
    fields_to_transfer = ["Hospital_Name", output_impedance_fieldname]
    for field in accumulate_attributes:
        fields_to_transfer.append("Total_" + field)
    arcpy.management.CopyFeatures(origins, output_features)
    arcpy.management.JoinField(output_features, "ID",
                                lines_sublayer, "Tract_ID", fields_to_transfer)

    print("Script completed successfully")

except Exception as e:
    # If an error occurred, print line number and error message
    import traceback, sys
    tb = sys.exc_info()[2]
    print("An error occurred on line %i" % tb.tb_lineno)
    print(str(e))

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html#derived-output

**text**　import json

network = r"C:/Data/SanFrancisco.gdb/Transportation/Streets_ND"
# Get all travel modes from the network dataset
travel_modes = arcpy.na.GetTravelModes(network)
# Get the Driving Distance travel mode
dd_travel_mode = travel_modes["Driving Distance"]
# Make a json representation of the travel mode
travel_mode_json = json.loads(str(dd_travel_mode))
# Modify the userHierarchy property to turn hierarchy off, and update the name
travel_mode_json["useHierarchy"] = False
travel_mode_json["name"] = "Driving Distance without Hierarchy"
# Create a new travel mode object from the modified json
new_travel_mode_object = arcpy.na.TravelMode(json.dumps(travel_mode_json))
# Use the new travel mode object to MakeODCostMatrixAnalysisLayer
# We could also pass in the json directly without first converting it to an object
arcpy.na.MakeODCostMatrixAnalysisLayer(network, "OD Without Hierarchy", new_travel_mode_object)

**id**　5c40e3e310f9c6065f-42

## 創建 OD 成本矩陣分析圖層 (Network Analyst 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**english**　Make OD Cost Matrix Analysis Layer

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html

**id**　5c40e3e310f9c6065f-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Make OD Cost Matrix Analysis Layer，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。網絡資料源 [network_data_source]、圖層名稱 （可選） [layer_name]、出行模式 （可選） [travel_mode]、中斷 （可選） [cutoff]、要查找的目的地數 （可選） [number_of_destinations_to_find]、時間 （可選） [time_of_day]、時區 （可選） [time_zone]、線狀 （可選） [line_shape]、累積屬性 （可選） [accumulate_attributes]、在求解時忽略無效的位置 （可選） [ignore_invalid_locations]、分析地理資料庫 （可選） [analysis_geodatabase]、分析圖徵資料集名稱 （可選） [analysis_feature_dataset_name]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-od-cost-matrix-analysis-layer.html#parameters
